| 63:0 | `write_data` | W | 0x0 a| Write data to mailbox fifo
|===

=== outbound_mailbox_0.READ_DATA

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]

| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:0 | `read_data` | R | - a| Read data from mailbox fifo
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_0-STATUS]
=== outbound_mailbox_0.STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:4 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 3 | `read_level_above_thresh` | R | 0x0 a| If set, read fifo level is higher than threshold set in RIRQT
| 2 | `write_level_above_thresh` | R | 0x0 a| If set, write fifo level is higher than threshold set in WIRQT
| 1 | `full` | R | 0x0 a| 0: Space is available to write, 1: Space is not available to write
| 0 | `empty` | R | 0x1 a| 0: Data is available to read, 1: Data is not available to read
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_0-ERROR_FLAGS]
=== outbound_mailbox_0.ERROR_FLAGS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:2 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 1 | `write_error` | R | 0x0 a| Attempted write to a full mailbox
| 0 | `read_error` | R | 0x0 a| Attempted read from an empty mailbox
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_0-WIRQT]
=== outbound_mailbox_0.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_0-RIRQT]
=== outbound_mailbox_0.RIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `rirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_0-IRQS]
=== outbound_mailbox_0.IRQS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:3 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 2 | `eirq` | RW | 0x0 a| On read:
[0]: No interrupt request
[1]: Error on mailbox access
On write:
[0]: No acknowledge
[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt request
| 1 | `rtirq` | RW | 0x0 a| On read:
[0]: No interrupt request
[1]: Usage level threshold in read mailbox exceeded
On write:
[0]: No acknowledge
[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt request
| 0 | `wtirq` | RW | 0x0 a| On read:
[0]: No interrupt request
[1]: Usage level threshold in write mailbox exceeded

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-inbound_mailbox_0-WIRQT]
=== inbound_mailbox_0.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-inbound_mailbox_2-WIRQT]
=== inbound_mailbox_2.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-inbound_mailbox_4-WIRQT]
=== inbound_mailbox_4.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_9-WIRQT]
=== outbound_mailbox_9.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:2 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 1 | `rflush` | W | 0x0 a| Flush the read FIFO for this port
| 0 | `wflush` | W | 0x0 a| Flush the write FIFO for this port
|===

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:0 | `write_data` | W | 0x0 a| Write data to mailbox fifo

=== outbound_mailbox_11.READ_DATA

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:0 | `read_data` | R | - a| Read data from mailbox fifo

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_11-STATUS]
=== outbound_mailbox_11.STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:4 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 3 | `read_level_above_thresh` | R | 0x0 a| If set, read fifo level is higher than threshold set in RIRQT
| 2 | `write_level_above_thresh` | R | 0x0 a| If set, write fifo level is higher than threshold set in WIRQT
| 1 | `full` | R | 0x0 a| 0: Space is available to write, 1: Space is not available to write
| 0 | `empty` | R | 0x1 a| 0: Data is available to read, 1: Data is not available to read
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_11-ERROR_FLAGS]
=== outbound_mailbox_11.ERROR_FLAGS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:2 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 1 | `write_error` | R | 0x0 a| Attempted write to a full mailbox
| 0 | `read_error` | R | 0x0 a| Attempted read from an empty mailbox
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_11-WIRQT]
=== outbound_mailbox_11.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_11-RIRQT]
=== outbound_mailbox_11.RIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `rirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_11-IRQS]
=== outbound_mailbox_11.IRQS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:3 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 2 | `eirq` | RW | 0x0 a| On read:
[0]: No interrupt request
[1]: Error on mailbox access
On write:
[0]: No acknowledge
[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt request
| 1 | `rtirq` | RW | 0x0 a| On read:
[0]: No interrupt request
[1]: Usage level threshold in read mailbox exceeded
On write:
[0]: No acknowledge
[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt request
| 0 | `wtirq` | RW | 0x0 a| On read:
[0]: No interrupt request
[1]: Usage level threshold in write mailbox exceeded
On write:
[0]: No acknowledge
[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt request
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_13-WIRQT]
=== outbound_mailbox_13.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-inbound_mailbox_17-WIRQT]
=== inbound_mailbox_17.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:2 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 1 | `rflush` | W | 0x0 a| Flush the read FIFO for this port
| 0 | `wflush` | W | 0x0 a| Flush the write FIFO for this port
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-inbound_mailbox_19-WRITE_DATA]
=== inbound_mailbox_19.WRITE_DATA

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:0 | `write_data` | W | 0x0 a| Write data to mailbox fifo
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-inbound_mailbox_19-READ_DATA]
=== inbound_mailbox_19.READ_DATA

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:0 | `read_data` | R | - a| Read data from mailbox fifo
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-inbound_mailbox_19-STATUS]
=== inbound_mailbox_19.STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:4 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 3 | `read_level_above_thresh` | R | 0x0 a| If set, read fifo level is higher than threshold set in RIRQT
| 2 | `write_level_above_thresh` | R | 0x0 a| If set, write fifo level is higher than threshold set in WIRQT
| 1 | `full` | R | 0x0 a| 0: Space is available to write, 1: Space is not available to write
| 0 | `empty` | R | 0x1 a| 0: Data is available to read, 1: Data is not available to read
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-inbound_mailbox_19-ERROR_FLAGS]
=== inbound_mailbox_19.ERROR_FLAGS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:2 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 1 | `write_error` | R | 0x0 a| Attempted write to a full mailbox
| 0 | `read_error` | R | 0x0 a| Attempted read from an empty mailbox
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-inbound_mailbox_19-WIRQT]
=== inbound_mailbox_19.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-inbound_mailbox_19-RIRQT]
=== inbound_mailbox_19.RIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `rirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-inbound_mailbox_19-IRQS]
=== inbound_mailbox_19.IRQS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:3 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 2 | `eirq` | RW | 0x0 a| On read:
[0]: No interrupt request
[1]: Error on mailbox access
On write:
[0]: No acknowledge
[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt request
| 1 | `rtirq` | RW | 0x0 a| On read:
[0]: No interrupt request
[1]: Usage level threshold in read mailbox exceeded
On write:
[0]: No acknowledge
[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt request
| 0 | `wtirq` | RW | 0x0 a| On read:
[0]: No interrupt request
[1]: Usage level threshold in write mailbox exceeded
On write:
[0]: No acknowledge
[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt request
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-inbound_mailbox_21-WIRQT]
=== inbound_mailbox_21.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_26-WIRQT]
=== outbound_mailbox_26.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:2 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 1 | `rflush` | W | 0x0 a| Flush the read FIFO for this port
| 0 | `wflush` | W | 0x0 a| Flush the write FIFO for this port
|===

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:0 | `write_data` | W | 0x0 a| Write data to mailbox fifo

=== outbound_mailbox_28.READ_DATA

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:0 | `read_data` | R | - a| Read data from mailbox fifo

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_28-STATUS]
=== outbound_mailbox_28.STATUS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:4 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 3 | `read_level_above_thresh` | R | 0x0 a| If set, read fifo level is higher than threshold set in RIRQT
| 2 | `write_level_above_thresh` | R | 0x0 a| If set, write fifo level is higher than threshold set in WIRQT
| 1 | `full` | R | 0x0 a| 0: Space is available to write, 1: Space is not available to write
| 0 | `empty` | R | 0x1 a| 0: Data is available to read, 1: Data is not available to read
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_28-ERROR_FLAGS]
=== outbound_mailbox_28.ERROR_FLAGS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:2 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 1 | `write_error` | R | 0x0 a| Attempted write to a full mailbox
| 0 | `read_error` | R | 0x0 a| Attempted read from an empty mailbox
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_28-WIRQT]
=== outbound_mailbox_28.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_28-RIRQT]
=== outbound_mailbox_28.RIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `rirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the fill pointer of the FIFO connected to the R channel exceeds this value, a read threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the read FIFO is full.
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_28-IRQS]
=== outbound_mailbox_28.IRQS

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:3 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 2 | `eirq` | RW | 0x0 a| On read:
[0]: No interrupt request
[1]: Error on mailbox access
On write:
[0]: No acknowledge
[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt request
| 1 | `rtirq` | RW | 0x0 a| On read:
[0]: No interrupt request
[1]: Usage level threshold in read mailbox exceeded
On write:
[0]: No acknowledge
[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt request
| 0 | `wtirq` | RW | 0x0 a| On read:
[0]: No interrupt request
[1]: Usage level threshold in write mailbox exceeded
On write:
[0]: No acknowledge
[1]: Acknowledge and clear interrupt request
|===

[#reg-{regmap-instance}-axil_mailbox_smc_wrap-outbound_mailbox_30-WIRQT]
=== outbound_mailbox_30.WIRQT

[cols="1,3,1,1,6", options="header"]
|===
| Bits | Field | Access | Reset | Description
| 63:8 | `Reserved` | - | - a| Reserved
| 7:0 | `wirqt` | RW | 0x0 a| When the usage pointer of the FIFO connected to the W channel exceeds this value, a write threshold IRQ is triggered and the corresponding STATUS register bit is set. When a value larger than or equal to the MailboxDepth parameter is written to this register, it gets reduced to MailboxDepth - 1 to ensure an IRQ is triggered when the write FIFO is full.
|===
